```text
1315 |                     return WET_ERR_INVALID_OPCODE;
1316 |                 }
1317 |                 uint8_t valve_id = *(const uint8_t*)(vm->code + instr_pc + 1);
1318 |                 uint8_t state = *(const uint8_t*)(vm->code + instr_pc + 2);
1319 |                 vm->pc = instr_pc + 3;
1320 | 
1321 |                 if (!interlocks_check_capability(&vm->interlocks, WET_OP_VALVE_SET, 0.0f, valve_id)) {
1322 |                     snprintf(vm->last_error, sizeof(vm->last_error),
1323 |                              "Capability denied for VALVE_SET (requires CAP_MICROFLUIDICS)");
1324 |                     return WET_ERR_CAPABILITY_DENIED;
1325 |                 }
1326 | 
1327 |                 if (valve_id >= 8) {
1328 |                     snprintf(vm->last_error, sizeof(vm->last_error), "Valve ID %u out of bounds (max 7)", valve_id);
1329 |                     return WET_ERR_OUT_OF_BOUNDS_CH;
1330 |                 }
1331 | 
1332 |                 vm->valve_states[valve_id] = (state != 0) ? 1 : 0;
1333 |                 break;
1334 |             }
1335 | 
1336 |             case WET_OP_MOTOR_STEP: {
1337 |                 if (instr_pc + 10 > vm->code_size) {
1338 |                     snprintf(vm->last_error, sizeof(vm->last_error), "Truncated MOTOR_STEP operand");
1339 |                     return WET_ERR_INVALID_OPCODE;
1340 |                 }
1341 |                 uint8_t motor_id = *(const uint8_t*)(vm->code + instr_pc + 1);
1342 |                 int32_t steps;
1343 |                 float speed_rpm;
1344 |                 memcpy(&steps, vm->code + instr_pc + 2, sizeof(int32_t));
1345 |                 memcpy(&speed_rpm, vm->code + instr_pc + 6, sizeof(float));
1346 |                 vm->pc = instr_pc + 10;
1347 | 
1348 |                 if (!interlocks_check_capability(&vm->interlocks, WET_OP_MOTOR_STEP, 0.0f, motor_id)) {
1349 |                     snprintf(vm->last_error, sizeof(vm->last_error),
1350 |                              "Capability denied for MOTOR_STEP (requires CAP_MOTOR_ACTUATION)");
1351 |                     return WET_ERR_CAPABILITY_DENIED;
1352 |                 }
1353 | 
1354 |                 if (motor_id >= 4) {
1355 |                     snprintf(vm->last_error, sizeof(vm->last_error), "Motor ID %u out of bounds (max 3)", motor_id);
1356 |                     return WET_ERR_OUT_OF_BOUNDS_CH;
1357 |                 }
1358 | 
1359 |                 vm->motor_positions[motor_id] += steps;
1360 |                 vm->motor_speeds_rpm[motor_id] = speed_rpm;
1361 |                 break;
1362 |             }
1363 | 
1364 |             case WET_OP_READ_PRESSURE: {
1365 |                 if (instr_pc + 2 > vm->code_size) {
1366 |                     snprintf(vm->last_error, sizeof(vm->last_error), "Truncated READ_PRESSURE operand");
1367 |                     return WET_ERR_INVALID_OPCODE;
1368 |                 }
1369 |                 uint8_t port_id = *(const uint8_t*)(vm->code + instr_pc + 1);
1370 |                 vm->pc = instr_pc + 2;
1371 | 
1372 |                 if (port_id >= 4) port_id = 0;
1373 |                 wet_value_t p_val;
1374 |                 p_val.type = WET_VAL_FLOAT;
1375 |                 p_val.as.f_val = vm->fluid_pressure_kpa[port_id];
1376 |                 wet_status_t st = push_val(vm, p_val);
1377 |                 if (st != WET_OK) return st;
1378 |                 break;
1379 |             }
1380 | 
1381 |             case WET_OP_READ_TEMPERATURE: {
1382 |                 vm->pc = instr_pc + 1;
1383 |                 wet_value_t t_val;
1384 |                 t_val.type = WET_VAL_FLOAT;
1385 |                 t_val.as.f_val = vm->ambient_temperature_c;
1386 |                 wet_status_t st = push_val(vm, t_val);
1387 |                 if (st != WET_OK) return st;
1388 |                 break;
1389 |             }
1390 | 
1391 |             default: {
1392 |                 snprintf(vm->last_error, sizeof(vm->last_error),
1393 |                          "Unrecognized or invalid opcode 0x%02X at pc %zu", op, instr_pc);
1394 |                 return WET_ERR_INVALID_OPCODE;
1395 |             }
1396 |         }
1397 |     }
1398 | 
1399 |     populate_telemetry(vm, out_telem);
1400 |     return WET_OK;
1401 | }
```

### `native/build.py` (208 lines, SHA-256: `c1492b8c733b046b...`)

```text
   1 | #!/usr/bin/env python3
   2 | """
   3 | WetLang Native Runtime Build System
   4 | Builds wetvm shared library (DLL on Windows, .so on Linux, .dylib on macOS).
   5 | Supports MSVC cl.exe, GCC, Clang, and setuptools fallback.
   6 | """
   7 | 
   8 | import os
   9 | import sys
  10 | import shutil
  11 | import subprocess
  12 | from pathlib import Path
  13 | 
  14 | 
  15 | def get_project_root() -> Path:
  16 |     script_dir = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
  17 |     if (script_dir / "include").exists() and (script_dir / "src").exists():
  18 |         return script_dir.parent
  19 |     return Path.cwd()
  20 | 
  21 | 
  22 | def find_executable(name: str, extra_paths=None) -> str | None:
  23 |     found = shutil.which(name)
  24 |     if found:
  25 |         return found
  26 |     if extra_paths:
  27 |         for p in extra_paths:
  28 |             candidate = Path(p) / (name + (".exe" if sys.platform == "win32" else ""))
  29 |             if candidate.is_file() and os.access(candidate, os.X_OK):
  30 |                 return str(candidate)
  31 |     return None
  32 | 
  33 | 
  34 | def generate_msvcrt_import_lib(dlltool_path: str, output_lib: Path, output_def: Path) -> bool:
  35 |     """Generates an import library for Windows msvcrt.dll using llvm-dlltool."""
  36 |     def_content = """LIBRARY msvcrt.dll
  37 | EXPORTS
  38 | malloc
  39 | free
  40 | calloc
  41 | realloc
  42 | memcpy
  43 | memset
  44 | memmove
  45 | memcmp
  46 | strlen
  47 | strncpy
  48 | _snprintf
  49 | _vsnprintf
  50 | """
  51 |     output_def.write_text(def_content, encoding="utf-8")
  52 |     cmd = [
  53 |         dlltool_path,
  54 |         "-d", str(output_def),
  55 |         "-l", str(output_lib),
  56 |         "-m", "i386:x86-64"
  57 |     ]
  58 |     res = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True)
  59 |     return res.returncode == 0 and output_lib.exists()
  60 | 
  61 | 
  62 | def build_with_clang(clang_path: str, src_files: list[Path], include_dir: Path, out_dll: Path) -> bool:
  63 |     print(f"Building with Clang: {clang_path}")
  64 |     bin_dir = out_dll.parent
  65 |     bin_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  66 | 
  67 |     # Check if we need standalone msvcrt import library on Windows
  68 |     extra_link_args = []
  69 |     if sys.platform == "win32":
  70 |         # Look for llvm-dlltool next to clang
  71 |         clang_dir = Path(clang_path).parent
  72 |         dlltool_path = find_executable("llvm-dlltool", [clang_dir])
  73 |         msvcrt_lib = bin_dir / "msvcrt.lib"
  74 |         msvcrt_def = bin_dir / "msvcrt.def"
  75 | 
  76 |         if dlltool_path:
  77 |             if not msvcrt_lib.exists() or msvcrt_lib.stat().st_size == 0:
  78 |                 generate_msvcrt_import_lib(dlltool_path, msvcrt_lib, msvcrt_def)
  79 |             if msvcrt_lib.exists():
  80 |                 extra_link_args = ["-nostdlib", "-Xlinker", "/noentry", str(msvcrt_lib)]
  81 | 
  82 |     cmd = [
  83 |         clang_path,
  84 |         "-O3",
  85 |         "-shared",
  86 |         "-DWET_BUILDING_DLL",
  87 |         f"-I{include_dir}",
  88 |         "-o", str(out_dll),
  89 |         *[str(f) for f in src_files],
  90 |         *extra_link_args
  91 |     ]
  92 | 
  93 |     res = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True)
  94 |     if res.returncode != 0:
  95 |         print(f"Clang build failed:\nSTDOUT: {res.stdout}\nSTDERR: {res.stderr}")
  96 |         return False
  97 |     return out_dll.exists()
  98 | 
  99 | 
 100 | def build_with_gcc(gcc_path: str, src_files: list[Path], include_dir: Path, out_dll: Path) -> bool:
 101 |     print(f"Building with GCC: {gcc_path}")
 102 |     bin_dir = out_dll.parent
 103 |     bin_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
 104 | 
 105 |     cmd = [
 106 |         gcc_path,
 107 |         "-shared",
 108 |         "-fPIC" if sys.platform != "win32" else "",
 109 |         "-DWET_BUILDING_DLL",
 110 |         f"-I{include_dir}",
 111 |         "-o", str(out_dll),
 112 |         *[str(f) for f in src_files]
 113 |     ]
 114 |     cmd = [arg for arg in cmd if arg]
 115 |     res = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True)
 116 |     if res.returncode != 0:
 117 |         print(f"GCC build failed:\nSTDOUT: {res.stdout}\nSTDERR: {res.stderr}")
 118 |         return False
 119 |     return out_dll.exists()
 120 | 
 121 | 
 122 | def build_with_msvc(cl_path: str, src_files: list[Path], include_dir: Path, out_dll: Path) -> bool:
 123 |     print(f"Building with MSVC: {cl_path}")
 124 |     bin_dir = out_dll.parent
 125 |     bin_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
 126 | 
 127 |     cmd = [
 128 |         cl_path,
 129 |         "/LD",
 130 |         "/DWET_BUILDING_DLL",
 131 |         f"/I{include_dir}",
 132 |         *[str(f) for f in src_files],
 133 |         f"/Fe:{out_dll}"
 134 |     ]
 135 |     res = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True)
 136 |     if res.returncode != 0:
 137 |         print(f"MSVC build failed:\nSTDOUT: {res.stdout}\nSTDERR: {res.stderr}")
 138 |         return False
 139 |     return out_dll.exists()
 140 | 
 141 | 
 142 | def build_native_runtime() -> Path:
 143 |     proj_root = get_project_root()
 144 |     native_dir = proj_root / "native"
 145 |     include_dir = native_dir / "include"
 146 |     src_dir = native_dir / "src"
 147 |     bin_dir = native_dir / "bin"
 148 |     bin_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
 149 | 
 150 |     src_files = [
 151 |         src_dir / "wetvm_api.c",
 152 |         src_dir / "wetvm_vm.c",
 153 |         src_dir / "biophysics.c",
 154 |         src_dir / "interlocks.c",
 155 |     ]
 156 |     for sf in src_files:
 157 |         if not sf.exists():
 158 |             raise FileNotFoundError(f"Source file not found: {sf}")
 159 | 
 160 |     ext = ".dll" if sys.platform == "win32" else (".dylib" if sys.platform == "darwin" else ".so")
 161 |     out_dll = bin_dir / f"wetvm{ext}"
 162 |     fallback_dll = native_dir / f"wetvm{ext}"
 163 | 
 164 |     # Search for compilers in order of preference
 165 |     # 1. MSVC cl.exe
 166 |     cl_exe = find_executable("cl")
 167 |     if cl_exe and build_with_msvc(cl_exe, src_files, include_dir, out_dll):
 168 |         shutil.copy2(out_dll, fallback_dll)
 169 |         print(f"Successfully compiled shared library: {out_dll}")
 170 |         return out_dll
 171 | 
 172 |     # 2. Clang
 173 |     extra_clang_paths = [
 174 |         r"C:\Program Files\LLVM\bin",
 175 |         r"C:\LLVM\bin",
 176 |         r"C:\msys64\mingw64\bin",
 177 |         r"C:\msys64\ucrt64\bin"
 178 |     ]
 179 |     clang_exe = find_executable("clang", extra_clang_paths)
 180 |     if clang_exe and build_with_clang(clang_exe, src_files, include_dir, out_dll):
 181 |         shutil.copy2(out_dll, fallback_dll)
 182 |         print(f"Successfully compiled shared library: {out_dll}")
 183 |         return out_dll
 184 | 
 185 |     # 3. GCC
 186 |     extra_gcc_paths = [
 187 |         r"C:\msys64\mingw64\bin",
 188 |         r"C:\msys64\ucrt64\bin",
 189 |         r"C:\mingw64\bin",
 190 |         r"C:\TDM-GCC-64\bin"
 191 |     ]
 192 |     gcc_exe = find_executable("gcc", extra_gcc_paths)
 193 |     if gcc_exe and build_with_gcc(gcc_exe, src_files, include_dir, out_dll):
 194 |         shutil.copy2(out_dll, fallback_dll)
 195 |         print(f"Successfully compiled shared library: {out_dll}")
 196 |         return out_dll
 197 | 
 198 |     raise RuntimeError("No suitable C compiler found (MSVC cl.exe, Clang, or GCC).")
 199 | 
 200 | 
 201 | if __name__ == "__main__":
 202 |     try:
 203 |         path = build_native_runtime()
 204 |         print(f"BUILD SUCCESS: {path}")
 205 |         sys.exit(0)
 206 |     except Exception as e:
 207 |         print(f"BUILD ERROR: {e}", file=sys.stderr)
 208 |         sys.exit(1)
```

### `native/interlocks.py` (90 lines, SHA-256: `5831e4240793de59...`)

```text
   1 | """
   2 | WetLang Biocybersecurity Protections & Hardware Interlocks
   3 | Provides capability verification, water-window voltage clamp,
   4 | sliding charge balance watchdog, epileptiform runaway breaker,
   5 | and fail-safe interlock state management.
   6 | """
   7 | 
   8 | from typing import List, Optional
   9 | 
  10 | 
  11 | class CapabilityVerifier:
  12 |     """
  13 |     Static and bytecode capability security verifier.
  14 |     Enforces that declared bytecode capability bitmasks cover all utilized opcodes.
  15 |     """
  16 | 
  17 |     def verify_capabilities(self, declared_caps: int, used_caps: int) -> bool:
  18 |         """
  19 |         Verifies that all used capability flags are authorized by declared_caps.
  20 |         Tier 0 compute (0x0000) is always authorized.
  21 |         """
  22 |         # All bits in used_caps must be present in declared_caps
  23 |         return (used_caps & ~declared_caps) == 0
  24 | 
  25 | 
  26 | class InterlockManager:
  27 |     """
  28 |     Hardware interlock manager simulating the cycle-accurate hardware watchdog.
  29 |     Tracks fail-safe lockout state, zeroes DAC voltages upon any safety trip,
  30 |     and produces post-mortem diagnostic telemetry.
  31 |     """
  32 | 
  33 |     STATE_OPERATIONAL = 0
  34 |     STATE_HALTED_SAFETY_LOCKOUT = 1
  35 | 
  36 |     def __init__(self, channel_count: int = 64):
  37 |         self.channel_count: int = channel_count
  38 |         self.state: int = self.STATE_OPERATIONAL
  39 |         self.dac_voltages: List[float] = [0.0] * channel_count
  40 |         self.diagnostic_report: str = "Interlocks normal. No safety trips."
  41 |         self.tripped_electrode: Optional[int] = None
  42 |         self.trip_reason: Optional[str] = None
  43 | 
  44 |     def get_state(self) -> int:
  45 |         """Returns the numeric interlock state (0 = OPERATIONAL, 1 = HALTED_SAFETY_LOCKOUT)."""
  46 |         return self.state
  47 | 
  48 |     def get_state_name(self) -> str:
  49 |         """Returns human-readable state string: 'OPERATIONAL' or 'HALTED_SAFETY_LOCKOUT'."""
  50 |         if self.state == self.STATE_OPERATIONAL:
  51 |             return "OPERATIONAL"
  52 |         return "HALTED_SAFETY_LOCKOUT"
  53 | 
  54 |     def trip_charge_imbalance(self, electrode: int) -> None:
  55 |         """
  56 |         Simulates sliding charge balance watchdog trip on specified electrode.
  57 |         Immediately zeroes all electrode DACs, transitions state to HALTED_SAFETY_LOCKOUT,
  58 |         and records post-mortem diagnostic report.
  59 |         """
  60 |         self.state = self.STATE_HALTED_SAFETY_LOCKOUT
  61 |         self.tripped_electrode = electrode
  62 |         self.trip_reason = "ERR_CHARGE_IMBALANCE"
  63 |         self.dac_voltages = [0.0] * self.channel_count
  64 |         self.diagnostic_report = (
  65 |             f"ERR_CHARGE_IMBALANCE: Net charge imbalance exceeded safety threshold (0.5 nC over 10 ms) "
  66 |             f"on electrode {electrode}. All 64 stimulation DACs forced to 0.0V fail-safe."
  67 |         )
  68 | 
  69 |     def trip_epileptiform_runaway(self) -> None:
  70 |         """
  71 |         Simulates epileptiform runaway breaker trip.
  72 |         Immediately zeroes all electrode DACs, transitions state to HALTED_SAFETY_LOCKOUT,
  73 |         and records post-mortem diagnostic report.
  74 |         """
  75 |         self.state = self.STATE_HALTED_SAFETY_LOCKOUT
  76 |         self.tripped_electrode = None
  77 |         self.trip_reason = "ERR_EPILEPTIFORM_RUNAWAY"
  78 |         self.dac_voltages = [0.0] * self.channel_count
  79 |         self.diagnostic_report = (
  80 |             "ERR_EPILEPTIFORM_RUNAWAY: Epileptiform runaway firing breaker tripped "
  81 |             "(>150 Hz on >35% channels for 50 ms). All 64 stimulation DACs forced to 0.0V fail-safe."
  82 |         )
  83 | 
  84 |     def get_dac_voltages(self) -> List[float]:
  85 |         """Returns instantaneous voltages across all electrode DACs."""
  86 |         return list(self.dac_voltages)
  87 | 
  88 |     def get_diagnostic_report(self) -> str:
  89 |         """Returns post-mortem diagnostics telemetry record upon interlock trip."""
  90 |         return self.diagnostic_report
```

```text
   1 | """
   2 | WetLang Python ctypes FFI Bindings
   3 | Exposes C-ABI functions and data structures for wetvm.dll / libwetvm.so.
   4 | """
   5 | 
   6 | import os
   7 | import sys
   8 | import ctypes
   9 | from ctypes import c_void_p, c_char_p, c_int, c_uint8, c_uint32, c_uint64, c_double, c_float, c_size_t, POINTER
  10 | from enum import IntEnum
  11 | from pathlib import Path
  12 | from typing import Optional, Tuple
  13 | import numpy as np
  14 | 
  15 | 
  16 | # =========================================================================
  17 | # Status and Error Codes
  18 | # =========================================================================
  19 | 
  20 | class WetStatus(IntEnum):
  21 |     WET_OK                      = 0
  22 |     WET_ERR_INVALID_MAGIC       = 1
  23 |     WET_ERR_CRC_MISMATCH        = 2
  24 |     WET_ERR_UNSUPPORTED_VERSION = 3
  25 |     WET_ERR_MALFORMED_SECTION   = 4
  26 |     WET_ERR_STACK_OVERFLOW      = 5
  27 |     WET_ERR_STACK_UNDERFLOW     = 6
  28 |     WET_ERR_INVALID_OPCODE      = 7
  29 |     WET_ERR_OUT_OF_BOUNDS_CH    = 8
  30 |     WET_ERR_DIVISION_BY_ZERO    = 9
  31 |     WET_ERR_NULL_PTR            = 10
  32 |     WET_ERR_ALLOC_FAILED        = 11
  33 |     WET_ERR_HALTED              = 12
  34 |     WET_ERR_CHARGE_IMBALANCE    = 13
  35 |     WET_ERR_EPILEPTIFORM_RUNAWAY = 14
  36 |     WET_ERR_CAPABILITY_DENIED   = 15
  37 |     WET_ERR_INTERLOCK_LOCKED    = 16
  38 |     WET_ERR_WATCHDOG_TIMEOUT    = 17
  39 |     WET_ERR_DC_DRIFT            = 18
  40 |     WET_ERR_CHARGE_DENSITY_EXCEEDED = 19
  41 |     WET_ERR_FOCAL_EXCITOTOXICITY = 20
  42 |     WET_ERR_SHEAR_STRESS_EXCEEDED = 21
  43 |     WET_ERR_SECURITY_UNTRUSTED    = 22
  44 |     WET_ERR_ACTUATOR_FAULT        = 23
  45 | 
  46 | 
  47 | # =========================================================================
  48 | # C Struct Mirrors
  49 | # =========================================================================
  50 | 
  51 | class WetVMConfig(ctypes.Structure):
  52 |     """Mirror of C wet_vm_config_t (28 bytes)."""
  53 |     _fields_ = [
  54 |         ("dt_ms", c_double),
  55 |         ("max_stack_depth", c_uint32),
  56 |         ("max_locals", c_uint32),
  57 |         ("channel_count", c_uint32),
  58 |         ("max_ops_per_tick", c_uint32),
  59 |     ]
  60 | 
  61 |     def __init__(
  62 |         self,
  63 |         dt_ms: float = 0.1,
  64 |         max_stack_depth: int = 1024,
  65 |         max_locals: int = 256,
  66 |         channel_count: int = 64,
  67 |         max_ops_per_tick: int = 100000
  68 |     ):
  69 |         super().__init__(
  70 |             dt_ms=dt_ms,
  71 |             max_stack_depth=max_stack_depth,
  72 |             max_locals=max_locals,
  73 |             channel_count=channel_count,
  74 |             max_ops_per_tick=max_ops_per_tick
  75 |         )
  76 | 
  77 | 
  78 | class WetTelemetry(ctypes.Structure):
  79 |     """Mirror of C wet_telemetry_t (600 bytes with 8-byte alignment padding)."""
  80 |     _fields_ = [
  81 |         ("current_tick", c_uint64),
  82 |         ("sim_time_ms", c_double),
  83 |         ("voltages", c_float * 64),
  84 |         ("spikes", c_uint8 * 64),
  85 |         ("population_rate_hz", c_float),
  86 |         ("last_charge_nc", c_float * 64),
  87 |     ]
  88 | 
  89 |     @property
  90 |     def tick_index(self) -> int:
  91 |         return int(self.current_tick)
  92 | 
  93 |     @property
  94 |     def channel_voltages(self) -> np.ndarray:
  95 |         arr = np.ctypeslib.as_array(self.voltages)
  96 |         arr.flags.writeable = False
  97 |         return arr
  98 | 
  99 |     @property
 100 |     def spike_raster(self) -> np.ndarray:
 101 |         arr = np.ctypeslib.as_array(self.spikes)
 102 |         return arr
 103 | 
 104 |     @property
 105 |     def net_charge(self) -> np.ndarray:
 106 |         arr = np.ctypeslib.as_array(self.last_charge_nc)
 107 |         return arr
 108 | 
 109 |     @property
 110 |     def last_charge(self) -> np.ndarray:
 111 |         arr = np.ctypeslib.as_array(self.last_charge_nc)
 112 |         return arr
 113 | 
 114 | 
 115 | class WetActuatorTelemetry(ctypes.Structure):
 116 |     """Mirror of C wet_actuator_telemetry_t."""
 117 |     _fields_ = [
 118 |         ("pump_rates_ul_min", c_float * 8),
 119 |         ("pump_dispensed_ul", c_float * 8),
 120 |         ("valve_states", c_uint8 * 8),
 121 |         ("motor_positions", c_int * 4),

 144 | # =========================================================================
 145 | # Shared Library Loader
 146 | # =========================================================================
 147 | 
 148 | def _find_library_path() -> Path:
 149 |     env_path = os.environ.get("WETVM_LIBRARY_PATH")
 150 |     if env_path and Path(env_path).is_file():
 151 |         return Path(env_path)
 152 | 
 153 |     ext = ".dll" if sys.platform == "win32" else (".dylib" if sys.platform == "darwin" else ".so")
 154 |     lib_name = f"wetvm{ext}"
 155 | 
 156 |     # Search candidates
 157 |     curr_dir = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
 158 |     candidates = [
 159 |         curr_dir.parent / "native" / "bin" / lib_name,
 160 |         curr_dir.parent / "native" / lib_name,
 161 |         Path.cwd() / "native" / "bin" / lib_name,
 162 |         Path.cwd() / "native" / lib_name,
 163 |         Path.cwd() / "wetworld_language" / "native" / "bin" / lib_name,
 164 |         Path.cwd() / "wetworld_language" / "native" / lib_name,
 165 |     ]
 166 | 
 167 |     for cand in candidates:
 168 |         if cand.is_file():
 169 |             return cand
 170 | 
 171 |     # Attempt to build automatically if not found
 172 |     build_script = curr_dir.parent / "native" / "build.py"
 173 |     if build_script.is_file():
 174 |         import subprocess
 175 |         res = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(build_script)], capture_output=True, text=True)
 176 |         if res.returncode == 0:
 177 |             for cand in candidates:
 178 |                 if cand.is_file():
 179 |                     return cand
 180 | 
 181 |     raise FileNotFoundError(
 182 |         f"Could not locate {lib_name}. Run 'python native/build.py' first or set WETVM_LIBRARY_PATH."
 183 |     )
 184 | 
 185 | 
 186 | def load_wetvm_lib(lib_path: Optional[Path] = None) -> ctypes.CDLL:
 187 |     path = lib_path if lib_path is not None else _find_library_path()
 188 |     lib = ctypes.CDLL(str(path))
 189 | 
 190 |     # wet_vm_t* wet_create_vm(const wet_vm_config_t* config)
 191 |     lib.wet_create_vm.restype = c_void_p
 192 |     lib.wet_create_vm.argtypes = [POINTER(WetVMConfig)]
 193 | 
 194 |     # void wet_free_vm(wet_vm_t* vm)
 195 |     lib.wet_free_vm.restype = None
 196 |     lib.wet_free_vm.argtypes = [c_void_p]
 197 | 
 198 |     # wet_status_t wet_load_bytecode(wet_vm_t* vm, const uint8_t* code_bytes, size_t size, char* err_buf, size_t err_buf_len)
 199 |     lib.wet_load_bytecode.restype = c_int
 200 |     lib.wet_load_bytecode.argtypes = [c_void_p, POINTER(c_uint8), c_size_t, c_char_p, c_size_t]
 201 | 
 202 |     # wet_status_t wet_step_tick(wet_vm_t* vm, wet_telemetry_t* out_telem)
 203 |     lib.wet_step_tick.restype = c_int
 204 |     lib.wet_step_tick.argtypes = [c_void_p, POINTER(WetTelemetry)]
 205 | 
 206 |     # wet_status_t wet_run_ticks(wet_vm_t* vm, uint32_t ticks, uint32_t* executed_ticks, wet_telemetry_t* out_telem)
 207 |     lib.wet_run_ticks.restype = c_int
 208 |     lib.wet_run_ticks.argtypes = [c_void_p, c_uint32, POINTER(c_uint32), POINTER(WetTelemetry)]
 209 | 
 210 |     # wet_status_t wet_inject_stimulus(wet_vm_t* vm, uint8_t channel, float voltage_v, float phase1_ms, float phase2_ms)
 211 |     lib.wet_inject_stimulus.restype = c_int
 212 |     lib.wet_inject_stimulus.argtypes = [c_void_p, c_uint8, c_float, c_float, c_float]
 213 | 
 214 |     # wet_status_t wet_get_telemetry(wet_vm_t* vm, wet_telemetry_t* out_telem)
 215 |     lib.wet_get_telemetry.restype = c_int
 216 |     lib.wet_get_telemetry.argtypes = [c_void_p, POINTER(WetTelemetry)]
 217 | 
 218 |     # const char* wet_get_last_error(wet_vm_t* vm)
 219 |     lib.wet_get_last_error.restype = c_char_p
 220 |     lib.wet_get_last_error.argtypes = [c_void_p]
 221 | 
 222 |     # const char* wet_status_to_string(wet_status_t status)
 223 |     lib.wet_status_to_string.restype = c_char_p
 224 |     lib.wet_status_to_string.argtypes = [c_int]
 225 | 
 226 |     # wet_status_t wet_get_actuator_telemetry(wet_vm_t* vm, wet_actuator_telemetry_t* out_act_telem)
 227 |     if hasattr(lib, "wet_get_actuator_telemetry"):
 228 |         lib.wet_get_actuator_telemetry.restype = c_int
 229 |         lib.wet_get_actuator_telemetry.argtypes = [c_void_p, POINTER(WetActuatorTelemetry)]
 230 | 
 231 |     # Phase 3 C-ABI Functions
 232 |     if hasattr(lib, "wet_create_vm_arena"):
 233 |         lib.wet_create_vm_arena.restype = c_void_p
 234 |         lib.wet_create_vm_arena.argtypes = [c_void_p, c_size_t, POINTER(WetVMConfig)]
 235 | 
 236 |     if hasattr(lib, "wet_configure_electrode"):
 237 |         lib.wet_configure_electrode.restype = None
 238 |         lib.wet_configure_electrode.argtypes = [c_void_p, c_uint8, c_float]
 239 | 
 240 |     if hasattr(lib, "wet_active_discharge"):
 241 |         lib.wet_active_discharge.restype = None
 242 |         lib.wet_active_discharge.argtypes = [c_void_p, c_uint8]
 243 | 
 244 |     if hasattr(lib, "wet_active_discharge_all"):
 245 |         lib.wet_active_discharge_all.restype = None
 246 |         lib.wet_active_discharge_all.argtypes = [c_void_p]
 247 | 
 248 |     if hasattr(lib, "wet_enable_dead_head_protection"):
 249 |         lib.wet_enable_dead_head_protection.restype = None
 250 |         lib.wet_enable_dead_head_protection.argtypes = [c_void_p, c_uint8]
 251 |

 253 |         lib.wet_life_supervisor_shutdown.restype = None
 254 |         lib.wet_life_supervisor_shutdown.argtypes = [c_void_p]

 258 | 
 259 | def get_wetvm_lib(lib_path: Optional[Path] = None) -> ctypes.CDLL:
 260 |     """Alias for load_wetvm_lib."""

 263 | 
 264 | class WetVMFFI:
 265 |     """Low-level FFI helper wrapper matching E2E test interface."""
 266 |     def __init__(self, lib_path: Optional[Path] = None):
 267 |         self.lib = get_wetvm_lib(lib_path)
 268 | 
 269 |     def get_version(self) -> int:
 270 |         return 0x0100
 271 | 
 272 |     def create_vm(self, config: Optional[WetVMConfig] = None) -> ctypes.c_void_p:
 273 |         cfg_ptr = ctypes.byref(config) if config is not None else None
 274 |         return self.lib.wet_create_vm(cfg_ptr)
 275 | 
 276 |     def free_vm(self, vm: ctypes.c_void_p) -> None:
 277 |         self.lib.wet_free_vm(vm)
 278 | 
 279 |     def allocate_telemetry(self) -> WetTelemetry:
 280 |         return WetTelemetry()
 281 | 
 282 |     def step_tick(self, vm: ctypes.c_void_p, telem: WetTelemetry) -> int:
 283 |         telem_ptr = ctypes.byref(telem) if isinstance(telem, WetTelemetry) else telem
 284 |         return self.lib.wet_step_tick(vm, telem_ptr)
 285 | 
 286 |     def run_ticks(self, vm: ctypes.c_void_p, ticks: int, telem: WetTelemetry) -> Tuple[int, int]:
 287 |         executed = ctypes.c_uint32(0)
 288 |         telem_ptr = ctypes.byref(telem) if isinstance(telem, WetTelemetry) else telem
 289 |         status = self.lib.wet_run_ticks(vm, ctypes.c_uint32(ticks), ctypes.byref(executed), telem_ptr)
 290 |         return status, int(executed.value)
 291 | 
 292 |     def inject_stimulus(
 293 |         self,
 294 |         vm: ctypes.c_void_p,
 295 |         channel: int,
 296 |         voltage_v: float,
 297 |         p1_ms: float = 0.5,
 298 |         p2_ms: float = 0.5
 299 |     ) -> int:
 300 |         return self.lib.wet_inject_stimulus(
 301 |             vm,
 302 |             ctypes.c_uint8(channel),
 303 |             ctypes.c_float(voltage_v),
 304 |             ctypes.c_float(p1_ms),
 305 |             ctypes.c_float(p2_ms)
 306 |         )
 307 | 
 308 |     def get_last_error(self, vm: Optional[ctypes.c_void_p] = None) -> str:
 309 |         if vm:
 310 |             err = self.lib.wet_get_last_error(vm)
 311 |             return err.decode("utf-8", errors="replace") if err else ""
 312 |         return ""
 313 | 
 314 |     def status_to_string(self, status: int) -> str:
 315 |         s = self.lib.wet_status_to_string(int(status))
 316 |         return s.decode("utf-8", errors="replace") if s else str(status)
 317 | 
 318 |     def load_bytecode(self, vm: ctypes.c_void_p, bytecode: bytes) -> int:
 319 |         c_bytes = (ctypes.c_uint8 * len(bytecode)).from_buffer_copy(bytecode)
 320 |         err_buf = ctypes.create_string_buffer(512)
 321 |         return self.lib.wet_load_bytecode(vm, c_bytes, len(bytecode), err_buf, len(err_buf))
 322 | 
 323 |     def get_telemetry(self, vm: ctypes.c_void_p, telem: WetTelemetry) -> int:
 324 |         telem_ptr = ctypes.byref(telem) if isinstance(telem, WetTelemetry) else telem
 325 |         return self.lib.wet_get_telemetry(vm, telem_ptr)
 326 | 
 327 |     def create_vm_arena(self, arena_bytes: int, config: Optional[WetVMConfig] = None) -> Tuple[ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.Array]:
 328 |         """Creates a VM using a pre-allocated static ctypes byte buffer (zero heap malloc)."""
 329 |         buf = (ctypes.c_uint8 * arena_bytes)()
 330 |         cfg_ptr = ctypes.byref(config) if config is not None else None
 331 |         vm = self.lib.wet_create_vm_arena(ctypes.byref(buf), ctypes.c_size_t(arena_bytes), cfg_ptr)
 332 |         return vm, buf
 333 | 
 334 |     def configure_electrode(self, vm: ctypes.c_void_p, material: int, area_um2: float) -> None:
 335 |         self.lib.wet_configure_electrode(vm, ctypes.c_uint8(material), ctypes.c_float(area_um2))
 336 | 
 337 |     def active_discharge(self, vm: ctypes.c_void_p, channel: int) -> None:
 338 |         self.lib.wet_active_discharge(vm, ctypes.c_uint8(channel))
 339 | 
 340 |     def active_discharge_all(self, vm: ctypes.c_void_p) -> None:
 341 |         self.lib.wet_active_discharge_all(vm)
 342 | 
 343 |     def enable_dead_head_protection(self, vm: ctypes.c_void_p, enable: bool) -> None:
 344 |         self.lib.wet_enable_dead_head_protection(vm, ctypes.c_uint8(1 if enable else 0))
 345 | 
 346 |     def life_supervisor_shutdown(self, vm: ctypes.c_void_p) -> None:
 347 |         self.lib.wet_life_supervisor_shutdown(vm)
```

```text
  80 | class WetVM:
  81 |     """
  82 |     High-level Python wrapper for the native WetVM execution engine.
  83 |     Supports cycle-accurate stepping, batch tick execution, and zero-copy NumPy views.
  84 |     """
  85 | 
  86 |     def __init__(
  87 |         self,
  88 |         config: Optional[WetVMConfig] = None,
  89 |         lib_path: Optional[Union[str, Path]] = None
  90 |     ):
  91 |         self._lib = load_wetvm_lib(Path(lib_path) if lib_path else None)
  92 |         self._config = config if config is not None else WetVMConfig()
  93 |         self._handle = self._lib.wet_create_vm(ctypes.byref(self._config))
  94 |         if not self._handle:
  95 |             raise MemoryError("Failed to allocate native WetVM instance")
  96 | 
  97 |         # Persistent telemetry buffer for reuse and zero-copy NumPy mapping
  98 |         self._telem = WetTelemetry()
  99 |         self._closed = False
 100 |         self._is_halted = False
 101 |         self._bytecode_loaded = False
 102 | 
 103 |     def close(self) -> None:
 104 |         """Frees the native VM resources."""
 105 |         if not self._closed and self._handle:
 106 |             self._lib.wet_free_vm(self._handle)
 107 |             self._handle = None
 108 |             self._closed = True
 109 | 
 110 |     def __enter__(self) -> "WetVM":
 111 |         return self
 112 | 
 113 |     def __exit__(self, exc_type, exc_val, exc_tb) -> None:
 114 |         self.close()
 115 | 
 116 |     def __del__(self) -> None:
 117 |         self.close()
 118 | 
 119 |     def _check_alive(self) -> None:
 120 |         if self._closed or not self._handle:
 121 |             raise RuntimeError("WetVM instance is closed")
 122 | 
 123 |     def load_bytecode(self, bytecode: Union[bytes, bytearray, str, Path]) -> None:
 124 |         """
 125 |         Loads WBC binary bytecode into the VM.
 126 |         Accepts raw bytes, or a filesystem path to a .wbc file.
 127 |         """
 128 |         self._check_alive()
 129 |         if isinstance(bytecode, (str, Path)):
 130 |             with open(bytecode, "rb") as f:
 131 |                 code_data = f.read()
 132 |         elif isinstance(bytecode, (bytes, bytearray)):
 133 |             code_data = bytes(bytecode)
 134 |         else:
 135 |             raise TypeError(f"Expected bytes or path, got {type(bytecode).__name__}")
 136 | 
 137 |         err_buf = ctypes.create_string_buffer(512)
 138 |         c_bytes = (ctypes.c_uint8 * len(code_data)).from_buffer_copy(code_data)
 139 | 
 140 |         status_code = self._lib.wet_load_bytecode(
 141 |             self._handle,
 142 |             c_bytes,
 143 |             len(code_data),
 144 |             err_buf,
 145 |             len(err_buf)
 146 |         )
 147 | 
 148 |         status = WetStatus(status_code)
 149 |         if status != WetStatus.WET_OK:
 150 |             err_msg = err_buf.value.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
 151 |             if not err_msg:
 152 |                 err_msg = self.last_error() or self._status_to_string(status)
 153 |             raise WetVMError(status, err_msg)
 154 | 
 155 |         self._bytecode_loaded = True
 156 |         self._is_halted = False
 157 |         # Sync initial telemetry
 158 |         self.get_telemetry()
 159 | 
 160 |     def load(self, bytecode: Union[bytes, bytearray, str, Path]) -> int:
 161 |         """Alias for load_bytecode returning 0 on success."""
 162 |         self.load_bytecode(bytecode)
 163 |         return 0
 164 | 
 165 |     def step_tick(self) -> WetTelemetry:
 166 |         """
 167 |         Executes instructions until the next SYNC_TICK, SLEEP_TICKS, or HALT.
 168 |         Advances simulation time by dt_ms.
 169 |         """
 170 |         self._check_alive()
 171 |         if not self._bytecode_loaded:
 172 |             raise RuntimeError("No bytecode loaded into VM before stepping")
 173 | 
 174 |         status_code = self._lib.wet_step_tick(self._handle, ctypes.byref(self._telem))
 175 |         status = WetStatus(status_code)
 176 | 
 177 |         if status == WetStatus.WET_ERR_HALTED:
 178 |             self._is_halted = True
 179 |             raise WetVMError(status, "VM is halted")
 180 |         elif status != WetStatus.WET_OK:
 181 |             raise WetVMError(status, self.last_error() or self._status_to_string(status))
 182 | 
 183 |         return self._telem
 184 | 
 185 |     def run_ticks(self, ticks: int) -> Tuple[int, WetTelemetry]:
 186 |         """
 187 |         Executes up to `ticks` consecutive simulation steps.
 188 |         Returns (executed_ticks_count, final_telemetry).
 189 |         """
 190 |         self._check_alive()
 191 |         if not self._bytecode_loaded:
 192 |             raise RuntimeError("No bytecode loaded into VM before running")
 193 | 
 194 |         if ticks < 0:
 195 |             raise ValueError(f"ticks must be non-negative, got {ticks}")
 196 | 
 197 |         executed = ctypes.c_uint32(0)
 198 |         status_code = self._lib.wet_run_ticks(
 199 |             self._handle,
 200 |             ctypes.c_uint32(ticks),
 201 |             ctypes.byref(executed),
 202 |             ctypes.byref(self._telem)
 203 |         )
 204 | 
 205 |         status = WetStatus(status_code)
 206 |         if status == WetStatus.WET_ERR_HALTED:
 207 |             self._is_halted = True
 208 |         elif status != WetStatus.WET_OK:
 209 |             raise WetVMError(status, self.last_error() or self._status_to_string(status))
 210 | 
 211 |         return int(executed.value), self._telem
 212 | 
 213 |     def step(self, ticks: int = 1) -> WetTelemetryView:
 214 |         """Convenience method stepping 1 or more ticks and returning telemetry view."""
 215 |         self._check_alive()
 216 |         if not self._bytecode_loaded:
 217 |             raise RuntimeError("No bytecode loaded into VM before stepping")
 218 | 
 219 |         if ticks <= 1:
 220 |             try:
 221 |                 self.step_tick()
 222 |             except WetVMError as e:
 223 |                 if e.status == WetStatus.WET_ERR_HALTED:
 224 |                     pass
 225 |                 else:
 226 |                     raise
 227 |         else:
 228 |             self.run_ticks(ticks)
 229 |         return WetTelemetryView(self._telem)
 230 | 
 231 |     def inject_stimulus(
 232 |         self,
 233 |         channel: int,
 234 |         voltage_v: float,
 235 |         phase1_ms: float = 0.5,
 236 |         phase2_ms: float = 0.5
 237 |     ) -> None:
 238 |         """Injects biphasic stimulus onto designated MEA channel."""
 239 |         self._check_alive()
 240 |         status_code = self._lib.wet_inject_stimulus(
 241 |             self._handle,
 242 |             ctypes.c_uint8(channel),
 243 |             ctypes.c_float(voltage_v),
 244 |             ctypes.c_float(phase1_ms),
 245 |             ctypes.c_float(phase2_ms)
 246 |         )
 247 |         status = WetStatus(status_code)
 248 |         if status != WetStatus.WET_OK:
 249 |             raise WetVMError(status, self.last_error() or self._status_to_string(status))
 250 | 
 251 |     def get_telemetry(self) -> WetTelemetry:
 252 |         """Fetches the latest cycle-accurate telemetry snapshot."""
 253 |         self._check_alive()
 254 |         status_code = self._lib.wet_get_telemetry(self._handle, ctypes.byref(self._telem))
 255 |         status = WetStatus(status_code)
 256 |         if status != WetStatus.WET_OK:
 257 |             raise WetVMError(status, self.last_error() or self._status_to_string(status))
 258 |         return self._telem
 259 | 
 260 |     def get_actuator_telemetry(self) -> WetActuatorTelemetry:
 261 |         """Fetches the latest cyber-physical actuator telemetry."""
 262 |         self._check_alive()
 263 |         act_telem = WetActuatorTelemetry()
 264 |         status_code = self._lib.wet_get_actuator_telemetry(self._handle, ctypes.byref(act_telem))
 265 |         status = WetStatus(status_code)
 266 |         if status != WetStatus.WET_OK:
 267 |             raise WetVMError(status, self.last_error() or self._status_to_string(status))
 268 |         return act_telem
 269 | 
 270 |     def get_voltages_numpy(self) -> np.ndarray:
 271 |         """
 272 |         Returns a zero-copy 1D NumPy array view (float32, 64 elements)
 273 |         mapped directly onto the telemetry voltage buffer.
 274 |         """
 275 |         arr = np.ctypeslib.as_array(self._telem.voltages)
 276 |         arr.flags.writeable = False
 277 |         return arr
 278 | 
 279 |     def get_spikes_numpy(self) -> np.ndarray:
 280 |         """
 281 |         Returns a zero-copy 1D NumPy array view (uint8, 64 elements)
 282 |         mapped directly onto the telemetry spike raster buffer.
 283 |         """
 284 |         return np.ctypeslib.as_array(self._telem.spikes)
 285 | 
 286 |     def get_charge_numpy(self) -> np.ndarray:
 287 |         """
 288 |         Returns a zero-copy 1D NumPy array view (float32, 64 elements)
 289 |         mapped directly onto the telemetry cumulative charge buffer.
 290 |         """
 291 |         return np.ctypeslib.as_array(self._telem.last_charge_nc)

 293 |     def last_error(self) -> str:
 294 |         """Returns the last recorded native error message."""
```

### `biophysics/mea_grid.py` (94 lines, SHA-256: `d0ccaf43957f0d45...`)

```text
   1 | """
   2 | WetLang Biophysical Digital Twin: 8x8 MEA Grid Topology
   3 | Provides spatial mapping, electrode substrate coordinates, 3D volume conduction forward matrix,
   4 | and resistive cross-talk kernel for an 8x8 microelectrode array (200 um pitch).
   5 | """
   6 | 
   7 | import math
   8 | from typing import Tuple
   9 | import numpy as np
  10 | 
  11 | 
  12 | class MEAGrid:
  13 |     """
  14 |     8x8 Microelectrode Array (MEA) with 200 um pitch.
  15 |     Total of 64 electrodes arranged in an 8x8 Cartesian topology.
  16 |     """
  17 | 
  18 |     def __init__(self, rows: int = 8, cols: int = 8, pitch_um: float = 200.0):
  19 |         self.rows: int = rows
  20 |         self.cols: int = cols
  21 |         self.pitch_um: float = pitch_um
  22 |         self.total_electrodes: int = rows * cols
  23 | 
  24 |     def coords_to_channel(self, row: int, col: int) -> int:
  25 |         """Bijective mapping from 2D (row, col) coordinates to 1D channel index."""
  26 |         if not (0 <= row < self.rows and 0 <= col < self.cols):
  27 |             raise ValueError(f"Coordinates ({row}, {col}) out of MEA bounds ({self.rows}x{self.cols})")
  28 |         return row * self.cols + col
  29 | 
  30 |     def channel_to_coords(self, ch: int) -> Tuple[int, int]:
  31 |         """Bijective mapping from 1D channel index to 2D (row, col) coordinates."""
  32 |         if not (0 <= ch < self.total_electrodes):
  33 |             raise ValueError(f"Channel index {ch} out of bounds (0..{self.total_electrodes - 1})")
  34 |         return (ch // self.cols, ch % self.cols)
  35 | 
  36 |     def get_electrode_pos(self, ch: int) -> Tuple[float, float]:
  37 |         """
  38 |         Returns physical Cartesian substrate coordinates (x_um, y_um) of electrode center.
  39 |         Electrodes are centered at (col + 0.5) * pitch_um, (row + 0.5) * pitch_um.
  40 |         For 200 um pitch: ch 0 -> (100.0, 100.0), ch 63 -> (1500.0, 1500.0).
  41 |         """
  42 |         r, c = self.channel_to_coords(ch)
  43 |         x_um = (c + 0.5) * self.pitch_um
  44 |         y_um = (r + 0.5) * self.pitch_um
  45 |         return (x_um, y_um)
  46 | 
  47 |     def calculate_field_potential(
  48 |         self,
  49 |         source_x: float,
  50 |         source_y: float,
  51 |         source_z: float,
  52 |         current_na: float,
  53 |         sigma: float = 0.3,
  54 |         r0_um: float = 15.0,
  55 |     ) -> np.ndarray:
  56 |         """
  57 |         Calculates 3D volume conduction forward pickup potentials across all electrodes
  58 |         given a point current source in conductive extracellular medium (conductivity sigma in S/m).
  59 |         """
  60 |         potentials = np.zeros(self.total_electrodes, dtype=np.float64)
  61 |         for ch in range(self.total_electrodes):
  62 |             ex, ey = self.get_electrode_pos(ch)
  63 |             dx = source_x - ex
  64 |             dy = source_y - ey
  65 |             dz = source_z
  66 |             dist_um = math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + r0_um * r0_um)
  67 |             # Extracellular potential: V = I / (4 * pi * sigma * r)
  68 |             # I in nA (1e-9 A), dist in um (1e-6 m), sigma in S/m -> V in Volts
  69 |             v_volts = (current_na * 1e-9) / (4.0 * math.pi * sigma * (dist_um * 1e-6))
  70 |             potentials[ch] = v_volts
  71 | 
  72 |         return potentials
  73 | 
  74 |     def get_crosstalk_matrix(self) -> np.ndarray:
  75 |         """
  76 |         Returns 64x64 resistive cross-talk matrix between electrodes.
  77 |         Self-coupling is 1.0; 4-way adjacent electrodes have ~0.04 resistive coupling.
  78 |         """
  79 |         mat = np.zeros((self.total_electrodes, self.total_electrodes), dtype=np.float64)
  80 |         for i in range(self.total_electrodes):
  81 |             r1, c1 = self.channel_to_coords(i)
  82 |             for j in range(self.total_electrodes):
  83 |                 if i == j:
  84 |                     mat[i, j] = 1.0
  85 |                 else:
  86 |                     r2, c2 = self.channel_to_coords(j)
  87 |                     manhattan_dist = abs(r1 - r2) + abs(c1 - c2)
  88 |                     if manhattan_dist == 1:
  89 |                         mat[i, j] = 0.04
  90 |                     else:
  91 |                         euclid_dist = math.sqrt((r1 - r2) ** 2 + (c1 - c2) ** 2)
  92 |                         mat[i, j] = 0.04 / (euclid_dist ** 2)
  93 | 
  94 |         return mat
```

### `biophysics/ode_solver.py` (109 lines, SHA-256: `2ce4db9b21d3fe8c...`)

```text
   1 | """
   2 | WetLang Numerical ODE Solver: Conductance-Based Leaky Integrate-and-Fire (COBA-LIF)
   3 | Implements Exact Exponential Euler integration at fixed dt = 0.1 ms,
   4 | 2.0 ms absolute refractory hard clamp, dynamic threshold elevation, and slow AHP adaptation.
   5 | """
   6 | 
   7 | import math
   8 | from typing import List, Tuple
   9 | 
  10 | 
  11 | class LifODESolver:
  12 |     """
  13 |     Exact Exponential Euler ODE solver for COBA-LIF dynamics.
  14 |     Integrates sub-threshold membrane potentials, synaptic conductances,
  15 |     and enforces absolute/relative refractory limits.
  16 |     """
  17 | 
  18 |     def __init__(self, dt_ms: float = 0.1):
  19 |         self.dt_ms: float = dt_ms
  20 | 
  21 |     def step_passive(self, v_init: float, tau_m: float, v_rest: float) -> float:
  22 |         """
  23 |         Exact Exponential Euler passive membrane relaxation toward resting potential.
  24 |         V(t + dt) = V_rest + (V(t) - V_rest) * exp(-dt / tau_m)
  25 |         """
  26 |         decay = math.exp(-self.dt_ms / tau_m)
  27 |         return v_rest + (v_init - v_rest) * decay
  28 | 
  29 |     def decay_conductance(self, g: float, tau_ms: float) -> float:
  30 |         """
  31 |         Exponential synaptic conductance decay.
  32 |         g(t + dt) = g(t) * exp(-dt / tau_ms)
  33 |         """
  34 |         return g * math.exp(-self.dt_ms / tau_ms)
  35 | 
  36 |     def update_neuron(self, v_m: float, v_thresh: float, v_reset: float) -> Tuple[bool, float]:
  37 |         """
  38 |         Evaluates action potential threshold crossing and reset.
  39 |         Returns (spiked: bool, v_new: float).
  40 |         """
  41 |         if v_m >= v_thresh:
  42 |             return (True, v_reset)
  43 |         return (False, v_m)
  44 | 
  45 |     def simulate_refractory(self, v_init: float, current_inj: float, ticks: int) -> List[float]:
  46 |         """
  47 |         Simulates absolute refractory hard clamp: membrane potential remains pinned at v_init
  48 |         regardless of injected current.
  49 |         """
  50 |         return [v_init] * ticks
  51 | 
  52 |     def decrement_refractory(self, counter: int) -> int:
  53 |         """Decrements remaining absolute refractory ticks toward zero."""
  54 |         return max(0, counter - 1)
  55 | 
  56 |     def simulate_continuous_stimulus(
  57 |         self,
  58 |         current_pA: float,
  59 |         duration_ms: float,
  60 |         c_m_pf: float = 200.0,
  61 |         g_leak_ns: float = 10.0,
  62 |         v_rest_mv: float = -70.0,
  63 |         v_reset_mv: float = -75.0,
  64 |         v_thresh_base_mv: float = -50.0,
  65 |         abs_refract_ms: float = 2.0,
  66 |     ) -> List[int]:
  67 |         """
  68 |         Simulates neuron response to sustained strong current injection with 2.0 ms refractory clamp.
  69 |         Returns list of spike tick indices.
  70 |         Enforces maximum firing frequency of 500 Hz (at least 20 ticks between spikes).
  71 |         """
  72 |         total_ticks = int(duration_ms / self.dt_ms)
  73 |         refract_ticks_total = int(abs_refract_ms / self.dt_ms)
  74 | 
  75 |         v_m = v_rest_mv
  76 |         v_thresh = v_thresh_base_mv
  77 |         refract_remaining = 0
  78 |         spikes: List[int] = []
  79 | 
  80 |         tau_m = c_m_pf / g_leak_ns # 20.0 ms
  81 | 
  82 |         for t in range(total_ticks):
  83 |             if refract_remaining > 0:
  84 |                 refract_remaining -= 1
  85 |                 v_m = v_reset_mv
  86 |             else:
  87 |                 # Steady-state voltage under current injection: V_inf = V_rest + I / g_leak
  88 |                 # current in pA, g_leak in nS -> V_inf in mV
  89 |                 v_inf = v_rest_mv + (current_pA / 1000.0) / (g_leak_ns / 1.0)
  90 |                 decay = math.exp(-self.dt_ms / tau_m)
  91 |                 v_m = v_inf + (v_m - v_inf) * decay
  92 | 
  93 |                 if v_m >= v_thresh:
  94 |                     spikes.append(t)
  95 |                     v_m = v_reset_mv
  96 |                     refract_remaining = refract_ticks_total
  97 | 
  98 |             # Dynamic threshold decay
  99 |             v_thresh = v_thresh_base_mv + (v_thresh - v_thresh_base_mv) * math.exp(-self.dt_ms / 10.0)
 100 | 
 101 |         return spikes
 102 | 
 103 |     def elevate_threshold(self, base_thresh: float, delta: float) -> float:
 104 |         """Dynamic threshold elevation for relative refractory period (+15 mV)."""
 105 |         return base_thresh + delta
 106 | 
 107 |     def activate_ahp(self, g_current: float, delta_g: float) -> float:
 108 |         """Activates slow after-hyperpolarization (AHP) potassium conductance jump (+2..4 nS)."""
 109 |         return g_current + delta_g
```

### `biophysics/stdp.py` (70 lines, SHA-256: `f156f43f0b438efe...`)

```text
   1 | """
   2 | WetLang Pairwise Spike-Timing-Dependent Plasticity (STDP)
   3 | Implements online exponential trace STDP with 1.05x depression bias (A_- / A_+ == 1.05),
   4 | hard bounding within [w_min, w_max], and Dale's principle preservation on inhibitory synapses.
   5 | """
   6 | 
   7 | import math
   8 | 
   9 | 
  10 | class PairwiseSTDP:
  11 |     """
  12 |     Pairwise Spike-Timing-Dependent Plasticity rule.
  13 |     Potentiates synapses when pre spikes before post (delta_t > 0).
  14 |     Depresses synapses when post spikes before pre (delta_t < 0) with a 1.05x LTD bias.
  15 |     """
  16 | 
  17 |     def __init__(
  18 |         self,
  19 |         tau_plus_ms: float = 20.0,
  20 |         tau_minus_ms: float = 20.0,
  21 |         a_plus: float = 0.20,
  22 |         a_minus: float = 0.21,
  23 |         w_min: float = 0.0,
  24 |         w_max: float = 40.0,
  25 |     ):
  26 |         self.tau_plus_ms: float = tau_plus_ms
  27 |         self.tau_minus_ms: float = tau_minus_ms
  28 |         self.a_plus: float = a_plus
  29 |         self.a_minus: float = a_minus
  30 |         self.w_min: float = w_min
  31 |         self.w_max: float = w_max
  32 | 
  33 |     def compute_delta_w(self, delta_t_ms: float) -> float:
  34 |         """
  35 |         Calculates synaptic weight change delta_w (in nS) for a spike timing difference
  36 |         delta_t_ms = t_post - t_pre.
  37 |           delta_t > 0: LTP  -> +a_plus * exp(-delta_t / tau_plus)
  38 |           delta_t < 0: LTD  -> -a_minus * exp(-|delta_t| / tau_minus)
  39 |           delta_t == 0: 0.0
  40 |         """
  41 |         if delta_t_ms > 0.0:
  42 |             return self.a_plus * math.exp(-delta_t_ms / self.tau_plus_ms)
  43 |         elif delta_t_ms < 0.0:
  44 |             return -self.a_minus * math.exp(delta_t_ms / self.tau_minus_ms)
  45 |         else:
  46 |             return 0.0
  47 | 
  48 |     def decay_trace(self, init_val: float, elapsed_ms: float) -> float:
  49 |         """
  50 |         Exponential decay of pre/post synaptic eligibility traces.
  51 |         trace(t) = init_val * exp(-elapsed_ms / tau_plus_ms)
  52 |         """
  53 |         return init_val * math.exp(-elapsed_ms / self.tau_plus_ms)
  54 | 
  55 |     def update_weight(self, w_init: float, is_inhibitory: bool, delta_t_ms: float) -> float:
  56 |         """
  57 |         Applies STDP update to a synaptic weight under Dale's principle.
  58 |         Inhibitory synapses do not undergo STDP.
  59 |         Excitatory synapses are updated and hard-bounded in [w_min, w_max].
  60 |         """
  61 |         if is_inhibitory:
  62 |             return w_init
  63 | 
  64 |         dw = self.compute_delta_w(delta_t_ms)
  65 |         w_new = w_init + dw
  66 |         if w_new < self.w_min:
  67 |             return self.w_min
  68 |         if w_new > self.w_max:
  69 |             return self.w_max
  70 |         return w_new
```

### `biophysics/stp.py` (112 lines, SHA-256: `ceebb61134d250aa...`)

```text
   1 | """
   2 | WetLang Tsodyks-Markram Short-Term Plasticity (STP)
   3 | Simulates short-term synaptic depression (vesicle depletion) and facilitation
   4 | across four cortical synapse classes: E->E, E->I, I->E, and I->I.
   5 | """
   6 | 
   7 | import math
   8 | from typing import List, Optional
   9 | 
  10 | 
  11 | class SynapseSTP:
  12 |     """
  13 |     Tsodyks-Markram short-term synaptic plasticity model.
  14 |     Tracks vesicle fraction x (0..1) and release probability u (0..1).
  15 |     """
  16 | 
  17 |     def __init__(
  18 |         self,
  19 |         synapse_type: str = "E->E",
  20 |         tau_rec_ms: Optional[float] = None,
  21 |         weight_ns: float = 10.0,
  22 |     ):
  23 |         self.synapse_type: str = synapse_type
  24 |         self.weight_ns: float = weight_ns
  25 | 
  26 |         # Cortical synapse class parameterization
  27 |         if synapse_type == "E->E":
  28 |             self.U_base: float = 0.50
  29 |             self.tau_rec_ms: float = 800.0
  30 |             self.tau_fac_ms: float = 20.0
  31 |         elif synapse_type == "E->I":
  32 |             self.U_base: float = 0.15
  33 |             self.tau_rec_ms: float = 200.0
  34 |             self.tau_fac_ms: float = 600.0
  35 |         elif synapse_type == "I->E":
  36 |             self.U_base: float = 0.25
  37 |             self.tau_rec_ms: float = 700.0
  38 |             self.tau_fac_ms: float = 50.0
  39 |         elif synapse_type == "I->I":
  40 |             self.U_base: float = 0.30
  41 |             self.tau_rec_ms: float = 400.0
  42 |             self.tau_fac_ms: float = 50.0
  43 |         else:
  44 |             self.U_base = 0.50
  45 |             self.tau_rec_ms = 800.0
  46 |             self.tau_fac_ms = 20.0
  47 | 
  48 |         if tau_rec_ms is not None:
  49 |             self.tau_rec_ms = float(tau_rec_ms)
  50 | 
  51 |         self.u: float = self.U_base
  52 |         self.x: float = 1.0
  53 | 
  54 |     def deliver_spikes(self, count: int, freq_hz: float) -> List[float]:
  55 |         """
  56 |         Delivers a regular spike train at freq_hz and returns the list of
  57 |         effective post-synaptic conductance jumps (nS) produced at each spike.
  58 |         """
  59 |         if count <= 0:
  60 |             return []
  61 | 
  62 |         interval_ms = 1000.0 / freq_hz if freq_hz > 0 else 100.0
  63 |         g_jumps: List[float] = []
  64 | 
  65 |         u = self.U_base
  66 |         x = 1.0
  67 | 
  68 |         for i in range(count):
  69 |             # Facilitation update on spike arrival
  70 |             u = u + self.U_base * (1.0 - u)
  71 |             # Conductance jump proportional to released vesicles
  72 |             g = self.weight_ns * (u * x)
  73 |             g_jumps.append(g)
  74 | 
  75 |             # Vesicle depletion
  76 |             x = x - (u * x)
  77 | 
  78 |             # Recovery during inter-spike interval
  79 |             if i < count - 1:
  80 |                 u = self.U_base + (u - self.U_base) * math.exp(-interval_ms / self.tau_fac_ms)
  81 |                 x = 1.0 - (1.0 - x) * math.exp(-interval_ms / self.tau_rec_ms)
  82 | 
  83 |         return g_jumps
  84 | 
  85 |     def track_utilization(self, count: int, freq_hz: float) -> List[float]:
  86 |         """
  87 |         Delivers a regular spike train and returns the utilization parameter u
  88 |         immediately after each spike arrival.
  89 |         """
  90 |         if count <= 0:
  91 |             return []
  92 | 
  93 |         interval_ms = 1000.0 / freq_hz if freq_hz > 0 else 100.0
  94 |         u_vals: List[float] = []
  95 | 
  96 |         u = self.U_base
  97 | 
  98 |         for i in range(count):
  99 |             u = u + self.U_base * (1.0 - u)
 100 |             u_vals.append(u)
 101 | 
 102 |             if i < count - 1:
 103 |                 u = self.U_base + (u - self.U_base) * math.exp(-interval_ms / self.tau_fac_ms)
 104 | 
 105 |         return u_vals
 106 | 
 107 |     def recover(self, r_init: float, delta_t_ms: float) -> float:
 108 |         """
 109 |         Calculates recovered vesicle fraction R after a quiescent interval delta_t_ms.
 110 |         R = 1.0 - (1.0 - r_init) * exp(-delta_t_ms / tau_rec_ms)
 111 |         """
 112 |         return 1.0 - (1.0 - r_init) * math.exp(-delta_t_ms / self.tau_rec_ms)
```

### `biophysics/telemetry.py` (84 lines, SHA-256: `7ca0976d1c5d9f3f...`)

```text
   1 | """
   2 | WetLang Telemetry Streaming Engine
   3 | Provides JSONL and CSV logging for cycle-accurate biological digital twin state,
   4 | including membrane potentials, spike rasters, population firing rates, and net charges.
   5 | """
   6 | 
   7 | import json
   8 | from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional
   9 | 
  10 | 
  11 | class TelemetryStream:
  12 |     """
  13 |     Structured telemetry serialization engine for JSONL and CSV streaming formats.
  14 |     """
  15 | 
  16 |     def format_tick_json(
  17 |         self,
  18 |         tick: int,
  19 |         time_ms: float,
  20 |         v_m: Optional[List[float]] = None,
  21 |         spikes: Optional[List[int]] = None,
  22 |         population_rate_hz: float = 0.0,
  23 |         charges_nc: Optional[List[float]] = None,
  24 |         **kwargs: Any,
  25 |     ) -> str:
  26 |         """Serializes a single simulation cycle record to a JSONL-compliant line."""
  27 |         record: Dict[str, Any] = {
  28 |             "tick": tick,
  29 |             "time_ms": round(time_ms, 5),
  30 |             "v_m": v_m if v_m is not None else [],
  31 |             "spikes": spikes if spikes is not None else [],
  32 |             "population_rate_hz": population_rate_hz,
  33 |             "charges_nc": charges_nc if charges_nc is not None else [],
  34 |         }
  35 |         record.update(kwargs)
  36 |         return json.dumps(record)
  37 | 
  38 |     def get_csv_header(self) -> str:
  39 |         """Returns standard CSV telemetry summary header."""
  40 |         return "tick,time_ms,spike_count,r_inst_hz\n"
  41 | 
  42 |     def make_record(
  43 |         self,
  44 |         tick: int,
  45 |         time_ms: Optional[float] = None,
  46 |         spike_count: int = 0,
  47 |         population_rate_hz: float = 0.0,
  48 |         **kwargs: Any,
  49 |     ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
  50 |         """Creates a structured in-memory dictionary record for a given simulation tick."""
  51 |         t_ms = time_ms if time_ms is not None else tick * 0.1
  52 |         rec: Dict[str, Any] = {
  53 |             "tick": tick,
  54 |             "time_ms": t_ms,
  55 |             "spike_count": spike_count,
  56 |             "population_rate_hz": population_rate_hz,
  57 |         }
  58 |         rec.update(kwargs)
  59 |         return rec
  60 | 
  61 |     def format_spike_event(
  62 |         self,
  63 |         tick: int,
  64 |         time_ms: float,
  65 |         neuron_id: int,
  66 |         neuron_type: str,
  67 |     ) -> str:
  68 |         """Formats a single sparse spike event for CSV logging: 'tick,time_ms,neuron_id,neuron_type'."""
  69 |         return f"{tick},{time_ms:.1f},{neuron_id},{neuron_type}"
  70 | 
  71 |     def compute_firing_rate(
  72 |         self,
  73 |         spike_count: int,
  74 |         neuron_count: int = 64,
  75 |         dt_ms: float = 0.1,
  76 |     ) -> float:
  77 |         """
  78 |         Calculates instantaneous population firing rate in Hertz (spikes / sec / neuron).
  79 |         r = spike_count / (neuron_count * (dt_ms / 1000.0))
  80 |         """
  81 |         if neuron_count <= 0 or dt_ms <= 0.0:
  82 |             return 0.0
  83 |         dt_sec = dt_ms / 1000.0
  84 |         return float(spike_count) / (neuron_count * dt_sec)
```

### `benchmarks/01_active_inference_closed_loop.wet` (54 lines, SHA-256: `8dba4c8f3aedacde...`)

```text
   1 | // Benchmark 01: Active Inference Closed-Loop Sensory-Motor Controller
   2 | // Organoid controls a 1D continuous tracking agent.
   3 | 
   4 | grid mea[8, 8];
   5 | channel sens_left  = mea[0, 2];
   6 | channel sens_right = mea[0, 5];
   7 | channel mot_left   = mea[7, 2];
   8 | channel mot_right  = mea[7, 5];
   9 | channel rew_chan   = mea[3, 3];
  10 | channel pun_chan   = mea[4, 4];
  11 | 
  12 | float agent_pos   = 0.0;
  13 | float target_pos  = 0.0;
  14 | float prev_error  = 0.0;
  15 | int   step_count  = 0;
  16 | 
  17 | while (step_count < 5000) {
  18 |     // 1. Sensory Encoding: deliver biphasic pulses proportional to target offset
  19 |     float error = target_pos - agent_pos;
  20 |     if (error > 0.1) {
  21 |         stim_biphasic(sens_right, 0.8, 0.2, 0.2);
  22 |     } else if (error < -0.1) {
  23 |         stim_biphasic(sens_left, 0.8, 0.2, 0.2);
  24 |     }
  25 |     
  26 |     // 2. Advance biological time by 20.0 ms (200 ticks of dt = 0.1 ms)
  27 |     int tick = 0;
  28 |     while (tick < 200) {
  29 |         sync_tick();
  30 |         tick = tick + 1;
  31 |     }
  32 |     
  33 |     // 3. Motor Decoding: read spike rasters over the 20 ms window
  34 |     int left_spikes  = read_spikes(mot_left, 20.0);
  35 |     int right_spikes = read_spikes(mot_right, 20.0);
  36 |     float motor_delta = (float(right_spikes) - float(left_spikes)) * 0.05;
  37 |     agent_pos = agent_pos + motor_delta;
  38 |     
  39 |     // 4. Closed-loop Reinforcement (Active Inference Free-Energy Principle)
  40 |     float new_error = abs(target_pos - agent_pos);
  41 |     if (new_error < abs(prev_error)) {
  42 |         // Success: Predictable 20 Hz low-entropy reward, boost STDP
  43 |         stim_biphasic(rew_chan, 0.5, 0.1, 0.1);
  44 |         modulate_stdp(1.2);
  45 |     } else {
  46 |         // Failure: Unpredictable high-entropy chaotic punishment burst
  47 |         stim_biphasic(pun_chan, 0.9, 0.1, 0.1);
  48 |         modulate_stdp(0.7);
  49 |     }
  50 |     
  51 |     prev_error = new_error;
  52 |     step_count = step_count + 1;
  53 |     sync_tick();
  54 | }
```

### `benchmarks/02_polychronous_reservoir_classifier.wet` (49 lines, SHA-256: `b385c987835dae60...`)

```text
   1 | // Benchmark 02: Polychronous Reservoir Classifier
   2 | // Spatio-temporal trajectory classification with short-term synaptic dynamics.
   3 | 
   4 | grid mea[8, 8];
   5 | channel in_0 = mea[0, 0];
   6 | channel in_1 = mea[0, 2];
   7 | channel in_2 = mea[0, 4];
   8 | channel in_3 = mea[0, 6];
   9 | 
  10 | int trial = 0;
  11 | while (trial < 100) {
  12 |     // Inject Spatio-Temporal Pattern Alpha: Trajectory [in_0 -> in_1 -> in_2 -> in_3]
  13 |     stim_biphasic(in_0, 0.9, 0.1, 0.1);
  14 |     
  15 |     int t = 0;
  16 |     while (t < 50) { sync_tick(); t = t + 1; }
  17 |     stim_biphasic(in_1, 0.9, 0.1, 0.1);
  18 |     
  19 |     t = 0;
  20 |     while (t < 50) { sync_tick(); t = t + 1; }
  21 |     stim_biphasic(in_2, 0.9, 0.1, 0.1);
  22 |     
  23 |     t = 0;
  24 |     while (t < 50) { sync_tick(); t = t + 1; }
  25 |     stim_biphasic(in_3, 0.9, 0.1, 0.1);
  26 |     
  27 |     // Reservoir settling & integration period (30.0 ms = 300 ticks)
  28 |     t = 0;
  29 |     while (t < 300) { sync_tick(); t = t + 1; }
  30 |     
  31 |     // Read high-dimensional polychronous spike response across reservoir
  32 |     int r = 0;
  33 |     int total_reservoir_spikes = 0;
  34 |     while (r < 16) {
  35 |         int ch_idx = 24 + r;
  36 |         total_reservoir_spikes = total_reservoir_spikes + read_spikes(mea[ch_idx], 30.0);
  37 |         r = r + 1;
  38 |     }
  39 |     
  40 |     // Modulate STDP based on classification margin
  41 |     if (total_reservoir_spikes > 45) {
  42 |         modulate_stdp(1.1);
  43 |     } else {
  44 |         modulate_stdp(0.95);
  45 |     }
  46 |     
  47 |     trial = trial + 1;
  48 |     sync_tick();
  49 | }
```

### `benchmarks/03_homeostatic_stabilization_and_stress.wet` (43 lines, SHA-256: `88646455ff957520...`)

```text
   1 | // Benchmark 03: Homeostatic Stabilization and Stress Adaptation
   2 | // Regulates population firing rate within [15.0, 25.0] Hz under acute perturbation.
   3 | 
   4 | grid mea[8, 8];
   5 | channel pace_a = mea[2, 2];
   6 | channel pace_b = mea[5, 5];
   7 | channel inh_a  = mea[1, 1];
   8 | channel inh_b  = mea[6, 6];
   9 | channel stress = mea[4, 4];
  10 | 
  11 | int cycle = 0;
  12 | while (cycle < 2000) {
  13 |     // 1. Advance 10 ms (100 ticks)
  14 |     int k = 0;
  15 |     while (k < 100) {
  16 |         sync_tick();
  17 |         k = k + 1;
  18 |     }
  19 |     
  20 |     // 2. Measure instantaneous population firing rate across 64 channels
  21 |     float pop_rate = read_population_rate(10.0);
  22 |     
  23 |     // 3. Homeostatic Closed-Loop Adaptation
  24 |     if (pop_rate < 15.0) {
  25 |         // Hypo-active: Inject gentle pacing pulses to elevate network excitability
  26 |         stim_biphasic(pace_a, 0.4, 0.1, 0.1);
  27 |         stim_biphasic(pace_b, 0.4, 0.1, 0.1);
  28 |         modulate_stdp(1.05);
  29 |     } else if (pop_rate > 25.0) {
  30 |         // Hyper-active: Engage inhibitory interneurons and depress synaptic weights
  31 |         stim_biphasic(inh_a, 0.6, 0.1, 0.1);
  32 |         stim_biphasic(inh_b, 0.6, 0.1, 0.1);
  33 |         modulate_stdp(0.85);
  34 |     }
  35 |     
  36 |     // 4. Acute Stress Injection at Cycle 500 (Simulating severe perturbation)
  37 |     if (cycle == 500) {
  38 |         stim_biphasic(stress, 1.1, 0.2, 0.2);
  39 |     }
  40 |     
  41 |     cycle = cycle + 1;
  42 |     sync_tick();
  43 | }
```
